(retVal);\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction intToString(number, decimalPlaces)\r\n{\r\n\tvar retVal = "";\r\n\r\n\tif(number !== undefined)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tif(number.length === undefined)\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tif(number (i < numLength); i++)\r\n\t\t\t{\r\n\t\t\t\tlet product = (quotent.toPrecision(numLength) * 10);\r\n\t\t\t\tretVal      += intToChar((product|0) + 48);\r\n\t\t\t\tquotent     =
